Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The p-adic variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 600 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 762 and intersection form of rank 3.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by p-adic generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 600, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 762. QED.

Theorem 213.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Given a p-adic algebra A over field F with structural decomposition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 229. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 621/4.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 229 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the p-adic structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 621. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
